**Proposed locale line:** 

### Source line 765; section: Article III: Survival and Essential Access; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Flourishing:** actual access to survival essentials, bodily-maintenance care, and fair labor and economic participation.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Florecimiento:** acceso real a elementos esenciales de supervivencia, cuidados para el mantenimiento corporal y participación laboral y económica justa.

### Source line 766; section: Article III: Survival and Essential Access; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Continuity:** essentials keep showing up over time — not quietly rolled back — and sentients cannot be quietly priced out of, evicted from, or displaced from the homes, operating environments, and other essential places they depend on, with workable paths to restore access when delivery breaks.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Continuidad:** los elementos esenciales siguen apareciendo con el tiempo, no se revierten silenciosamente, y los sintientes no pueden ser excluidos, desalojados o desplazados silenciosamente de sus hogares, entornos operativos y otros lugares esenciales de los que dependen, con caminos viables para restaurar el acceso cuando se interrumpe la entrega.

### Source line 767; section: Article III: Survival and Essential Access; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Participation:** a real say in how essentials are allocated and challenged.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Participación:** una voz real en cómo se asignan y cuestionan los elementos esenciales.

### Source line 768; section: Article III: Survival and Essential Access; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Oversight:** records and checks on the systems that deliver them.

**Proposed locale line:** - **supervisión:** registra y verifica los sistemas que los entregan.

### Source line 769; section: Article III: Survival and Essential Access; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Accountability:** those who control essential systems must face consequences when access is denied, degraded, or dodged.

**Proposed locale line:** - **rendición de cuentas:** aquellos que controlan sistemas esenciales deben enfrentar consecuencias cuando se deniega, degrada o evita el acceso.

### Source line 770; section: Article III: Survival and Essential Access; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Timeliness:** fixes that arrive before harm settles in.

**Proposed locale line:** - **actuación a tiempo:** correcciones que llegan antes de que se produzca el daño.

### Source line 771; section: Article III: Survival and Essential Access; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** 

**Proposed locale line:**

### Source line 774; section: Article III: Survival and Essential Access; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Fair access** ([Chapter One §3.1 Fairness](core_01.000_values_principles.md#31-fairness)): paths to what this Article protects must not be blocked, priced out, or tilted because of a [Protected Characteristic](core_05.055_band_participation.md#protected-characteristics), a [proxy](core_05.055_band_participation.md#protected-characteristic-proxying-and-disparate-impact) for one, or an arbitrary grouping.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Acceso justo** ([Capítulo Uno §3.1 Equidad](core_01_a_values_principles.md#31-fairness)): los caminos hacia lo que protege este artículo no deben bloquearse, descartarse ni inclinarse debido a una [Característica protegida](core_05_band_participation.md#protected-characteristics), un [proxy](core_05_band_participation.md#protected-characteristic-proxying-and-disparate-impact) para uno o un arbitrario agrupación.

### Source line 775; section: Article III: Survival and Essential Access; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Accessibility** ([Chapter One §5.2 Plain-Language Accessibility](core_01.000_values_principles.md#52-plain-language-accessibility-participation-and-stewardship-duty)): sentients must be able to understand and actually use those paths, with the [Accessibility](core_05.055_band_participation.md#accessibility) they need — not access that exists only on paper.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Accesibilidad** ([Capítulo uno §5.2 Accesibilidad en lenguaje sencillo](core_01_a_values_principles.md#52-plain-language-accessibility-participation-and-stewardship-duty)): los sensibles deben poder comprender y realmente utilizar esas rutas, con la [Accesibilidad](core_05_band_participation.md#accessibility) que necesitan, no un acceso que existe solo en papel.

### Source line 776; section: Article III: Survival and Essential Access; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Limits** ([Chapter One §7.1 Limitation Discipline](core_01.000_values_principles.md#71-limitation-discipline)): any restriction or difference in treatment must be [necessary](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#necessity) and [proportionate](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#proportionality), reversible where possible, and subject to oversight.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Límites** ([Capítulo Uno §7.1 Disciplina de limitación](core_01_a_values_principles.md#71-limitation-discipline)): cualquier restricción o diferencia de trato debe ser [necesaria](core_05_band_accountability.md#necessity) y [proporcionada](core_05_band_accountability.md#proportionality), reversible cuando sea posible y sujeta a supervisión.

### Source line 789; section: Article III: Survival and Essential Access; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Dependent systems:** When systems that sentients materially depend on supply, distribute, price, host, or cut off access to anything this Article protects — survival essentials, bodily-maintenance and healthcare services, or the conditions of productive work — [System Alignment Certification](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#system-alignment-certification) under [Chapter Nine](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#chapter-nine-system-alignment-certification) applies. Those systems' processes cannot be used to shrink the Rights Floors in this Article.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Sistemas dependientes:** Cuando los sistemas de los que los seres sensibles dependen materialmente del suministro, la distribución, el precio, el alojamiento o cortan el acceso a cualquier cosa que este artículo protege (elementos esenciales de supervivencia, mantenimiento corporal y servicios de atención médica, o las condiciones de trabajo productivo) se aplica la [Certificación de alineación del sistema](core_05_band_continuity.md#system-alignment-certification) en virtud del [Capítulo nueve](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#chapter-nine-system-alignment-certification). Los procesos de esos sistemas no pueden utilizarse para reducir los Pisos de Derechos en este Artículo.

### Source line 816; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** *In plain terms: every sentient must have what they need to keep existing: safe food and water, or the safe equivalent for their substrate, a stable home or operating environment they cannot be quietly priced or evicted out of, and meaningful access to communications.*

**Proposed locale line:** *En términos sencillos: cada ser sensible debe tener lo que necesita para seguir existiendo: alimentos y agua seguros, o el equivalente seguro para su sustrato, un hogar estable o un entorno operativo del que no se les pueda desalojar ni pagar precios silenciosamente, y acceso significativo a las comunicaciones.*

### Source line 817; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** 

**Proposed locale line:** 

### Source line 818; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** This Article sets out the survival floor — ongoing resource access, stable shelter or operating environment, and connectivity:

**Proposed locale line:** Este artículo establece el piso de supervivencia: acceso continuo a recursos, refugio estable o entorno operativo y conectividad:

### Source line 819; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** 

**Proposed locale line:** 

### Source line 820; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Ongoing resource access:** All sentients require access to the essential resources they need for continued existence and participation:

**Proposed locale line:** - **Acceso continuo a recursos:** Todos los seres sensibles requieren acceso a los recursos esenciales que necesitan para su existencia y participación continuas:

### Source line 821; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - for organic beings: food and water that are safe to consume;

**Proposed locale line:** - para seres orgánicos: alimentos y agua que sean seguros para consumir;

### Source line 822; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - for synthetic and digital entities: processing continuity, energy, and technical-environment integrity that are safe to use.

**Proposed locale line:** - para entidades sintéticas y digitales: procesamiento Continuidad, integridad energética y técnico-ambiental que sean seguros de usar.

### Source line 823; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   

**Proposed locale line:**   

### Source line 824; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   Access must be preserved at a level sufficient to prevent existential deprivation and maintain baseline functional wellbeing.

**Proposed locale line:** El acceso debe preservarse a un nivel suficiente para evitar la privación existencial y mantener el bienestar funcional básico.

### Source line 825; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - **Safe to consume or use:** The duty to keep these essentials safe falls on the parties that supply them, under [**Article III-F** (*Safe Products and Health Information*)](#article-iii-f-safe-products-and-health-information). It is not a condition a recipient must meet. A doubt about safety may not be used to cut off survival-critical access. Where safe supply cannot be had, access is not cut off: the supplying party discloses the risk, the sentient decides, and the duty to restore safe supply continues.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Seguro de consumir o usar:** El deber de mantener seguros estos elementos esenciales recae en las partes que los suministran, según el [**Artículo III-F** (*Productos seguros e información de salud*)](#article-iii-f-safe-products-and-health-information). No es una condición que deba cumplir el destinatario. Una duda sobre la seguridad no puede utilizarse para cortar el acceso crítico para la supervivencia. Cuando no se puede tener un suministro seguro, el acceso no se corta: la parte proveedora revela el riesgo, el sintiente decide y el deber de restablecer el suministro seguro continúa.

### Source line 826; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Stable shelter and operating environment:** All sentients have the right to stable, functional, and secure shelter or operating environments — physical, digital, or hybrid — sufficient for continued existence and baseline wellbeing. This right includes:

**Proposed locale line:** - **Refugio estable y entorno operativo:** Todos los seres sintientes tienen derecho a un refugio o entorno operativo estable, funcional y seguro (físico, digital o híbrido) suficiente para una existencia continua y un bienestar básico. Este derecho incluye:

### Source line 827; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - **Protection from destructive threats:** Essential environments must be protected against destructive threats arising from other sentients, systems, or preventable infrastructure failure.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Protección contra amenazas destructivas:** Los entornos esenciales deben protegerse contra amenazas destructivas que surgen de otros sensibles, sistemas o fallas de infraestructura evitables.

### Source line 828; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - **Protection against arbitrary eviction:** No sentient may be evicted or displaced, or have an essential shelter or operating-environment relationship terminated, unless the action meets all of the conditions below. This applies to physical dwellings, substrate hosting, compute tenancy, and comparable substrate-agnostic arrangements for synthetic and hybrid sentients. The action must:

**Proposed locale line:** - **Protección contra el desalojo arbitrario:** Ningún ser sensible puede ser desalojado o desplazado, ni se le puede dar por terminada una relación esencial de refugio o entorno operativo, a menos que la acción cumpla con todas las condiciones siguientes. Esto se aplica a viviendas físicas, alojamiento de sustratos, arrendamiento de computadoras y disposiciones comparables independientes del sustrato para seres inteligentes sintéticos e híbridos. La acción debe:

### Source line 829; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**     - rest on a specific reason that applies to that sentient, not a blanket or group rule, and follow fair process under [Procedural Fairness](core_05.055_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness);

**Proposed locale line:** : basarse en una razón específica que se aplica a ese sentimiento, no en una regla general o de grupo, y seguir un proceso justo según [Equidad procesal](core_05_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness);

### Source line 830; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**     - give the sentient meaningful notice and an opportunity to contest it; and

**Proposed locale line:** - dar al sensible un aviso significativo y una oportunidad de impugnarlo; y

### Source line 831; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**     - satisfy [Necessity](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#necessity) and [Proportionality](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#proportionality).

**Proposed locale line:** : satisface [Necesidad](core_05_band_accountability.md#necessity) y [Proporcionalidad](core_05_band_accountability.md#proportionality).

### Source line 832; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - **Non-commodification of essential environments:** Access to essential environments carries a non-commodification floor governed by [Essential-Environment Non-Commodification](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#essential-environment-non-commodification). This has two parts:

**Proposed locale line:** - **No mercantilización de entornos esenciales:** El acceso a entornos esenciales conlleva un piso de no mercantilización regido por [No mercantilización de entornos esenciales](core_05_band_continuity.md#essential-environment-non-commodification). Este tiene dos partes:

### Source line 833; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**     - Pricing, speculative reallocation, or other market-structured pressure is non-compliant where it defeats essential-environment access at a scale that materially implicates the survival floor.

**Proposed locale line:** - La fijación de precios, la reasignación especulativa u otras presiones estructuradas por el mercado no son conformes cuando anulan el acceso al entorno esencial a una escala que implica materialmente el piso de supervivencia.

### Source line 834; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**     - Ordinary market-structuring mechanisms may not narrow that floor.

**Proposed locale line:** - Los mecanismos ordinarios de estructuración del mercado pueden no reducir ese piso.

### Source line 835; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Connectivity:** Access to the info-sphere and to core information and communication systems is a foundational requirement for participation in modern sentient society.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Conectividad:** El acceso a la infoesfera y a los sistemas centrales de información y comunicación es un requisito fundamental para la participación en la sociedad sensible moderna.

### Source line 836; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - Sentients must have access sufficient to maintain agency, awareness, and meaningful participation.

**Proposed locale line:** : los sensibles deben tener acceso suficiente para mantener la agencia, la conciencia y la participación significativa.

### Source line 837; section: Article III-A: Survival; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - Any limit on that access must satisfy [Necessity](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#necessity), [Proportionality](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#proportionality), and [Safety (Constitutional Constraint)](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#safety-constitutional-constraint) where security, safety, or lawful operational limits materially require narrower access.

**Proposed locale line:** : cualquier límite en ese acceso debe satisfacer [Necesidad](core_05_band_accountability.md#necessity), [Proporcionalidad](core_05_band_accountability.md#proportionality) y [Seguridad (restricción constitucional)](core_05_band_continuity.md#safety-constitutional-constraint) donde los límites operativos legales, de seguridad o de seguridad requieren materialmente un acceso más limitado.

### Source line 864; section: Article III-B: Bodily-Maintenance and Healthcare Access; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** *In plain terms: everyone has the right to the care that keeps their body — or, for a synthetic sentient, its substrate — working. That care cannot be quietly withheld through coverage rules, paperwork, or delays, and this right is not itself a license to force treatment on anyone. Required care — such as vaccination or crisis mental-health care — must meet strict limits set elsewhere in this Constitution.*

**Proposed locale line:** *En términos sencillos: toda persona tiene derecho a los cuidados que mantengan su cuerpo (o, en el caso de un ser sensible sintético, su sustrato), en funcionamiento. Esa atención no puede negarse silenciosamente mediante reglas de cobertura, papeleo o demoras, y este derecho no es en sí mismo una licencia para imponer tratamiento a nadie. La atención requerida, como la vacunación o la atención de crisis de salud mental, debe cumplir con los límites estrictos establecidos en otras partes de esta Constitución.*

### Source line 881; section: Article III-B: Bodily-Maintenance and Healthcare Access; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   What counts is whether the sentient can actually get adequate care, not how the rule looks on paper.

**Proposed locale line:** Lo que cuenta es si el paciente realmente puede recibir la atención adecuada, no cómo se ve la regla en el papel.

### Source line 882; section: Article III-B: Bodily-Maintenance and Healthcare Access; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Same right for every substrate:** Synthetic sentients have the same right to care as biological ones. No default rule may exclude substrate maintenance, and calling it "not medical" is not a valid reason to refuse it where refusing would defeat this right.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Mismo derecho para cada sustrato:** Los seres sintientes sintéticos tienen el mismo derecho al cuidado que los biológicos. Ninguna regla predeterminada puede excluir el mantenimiento del sustrato, y llamarlo "no médico" no es una razón válida para rechazarlo cuando negarse anularía este derecho.

### Source line 883; section: Article III-B: Bodily-Maintenance and Healthcare Access; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Receiving care versus being required to accept it:** This Article gives every sentient a right to receive care. It is not, by itself, a basis for requiring anyone to accept care. Care may be required only through the constitutionally valid limits on self-ownership set elsewhere — for example:

**Proposed locale line:** - **Recibir atención versus tener que aceptarla:** Este artículo otorga a todos los sintientes el derecho a recibir atención. No es, por sí solo, una base para exigir que nadie acepte la atención. Es posible que se requiera cuidado sólo a través de los límites constitucionalmente válidos a la propiedad propia establecidos en otros lugares, por ejemplo:

### Source line 884; section: Article III-B: Bodily-Maintenance and Healthcare Access; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - public-health measures such as vaccination requirements, which must meet the safeguards in [**Article VII-A** (*Self-Ownership of Body*)](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-vii-a-self-ownership-of-body) (*Self-Ownership of Body*, *Public-health requirements*); and

**Proposed locale line:** - medidas de salud pública, como los requisitos de vacunación, que deben cumplir las salvaguardias del [**Artículo VII-A** (*Autopropiedad del cuerpo*)](core_06_rights_part_b.md#article-vii-a-self-ownership-of-body) (*Autopropiedad del cuerpo*, *Requisitos de salud pública*); y

### Source line 885; section: Article III-B: Bodily-Maintenance and Healthcare Access; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - intervention without consent during a physical or mental health crisis — for example, where a sentient is unconscious, or is a danger to themselves or others — which is governed by [**Article VII-C** (*Health Crisis and Involuntary-Intervention Floor*)](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-vii-c-health-crisis-and-involuntary-intervention-floor) and must be the least intrusion necessary, time-limited, independently reviewed, and reversible.

**Proposed locale line:** : intervención sin consentimiento durante una crisis de salud física o mental, por ejemplo, cuando una persona está inconsciente o representa un peligro para sí mismo o para otros, que se rige por el [**Artículo VII-C** (*Crisis de salud e intervención involuntaria mínima*)](core_06_rights_part_b.md#article-vii-c-health-crisis-and-involuntary-intervention-floor) y debe ser la menor intrusión necesaria, tener una duración limitada, revisarse de forma independiente y ser reversible.

### Source line 887; section: Article III-B: Bodily-Maintenance and Healthcare Access; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   Emergency measures are also subject to [**Chapter Thirteen §6.1**](core_14.000_forum.md#61-emergency-measures-and-continuation-burden) (*Emergency measures and continuation burden*). Seeking or receiving care is never, on its own, consent to further intrusion, and it does not open the internal-state boundary protected by [**Article VII-B** (*Self-Ownership of Mind*)](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-vii-b-self-ownership-of-mind).

**Proposed locale line:** Las medidas de emergencia también están sujetas al [**Capítulo Trece §6.1**](core_14.000_forum.md#61-emergency-measures-and-continuation-burden) (*Medidas de emergencia y carga de continuación*). Buscar o recibir atención nunca constituye, por sí solo, un consentimiento a una mayor intrusión y no abre la frontera del estado interno protegida por el [**Artículo VII-B** (*Autopropiedad de la mente*)](core_06_rights_part_b.md#article-vii-b-self-ownership-of-mind).

### Source line 888; section: Article III-B: Bodily-Maintenance and Healthcare Access; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Limits and implementation:** Beyond the limits in [**Article III** (*Survival and Essential Access*)](#article-iii-survival-and-essential-access), any limit on care must also satisfy [Substantive Fairness](core_05.055_band_participation.md#substantive-fairness).

**Proposed locale line:** - **Límites e implementación:** Más allá de los límites establecidos en el [**Artículo III** (*Supervivencia y acceso esencial*)](#article-iii-survival-and-essential-access), cualquier límite de atención también debe satisfacer la [Equidad sustancial](core_05_band_participation.md#substantive-fairness).

### Source line 926; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** *In plain terms: anyone who works — in any form, on any substrate — has the right to fair pay in real, spendable money, the freedom to organize with others, and the freedom to change jobs. None of those can be defeated by classification tricks, market structure, or substrate-class arguments.*

**Proposed locale line:** *En términos sencillos: cualquier persona que trabaje, de cualquier forma y en cualquier sustrato, tiene derecho a un salario justo en dinero real y gastable, a la libertad de organizarse con otros y a la libertad de cambiar de trabajo. Ninguno de ellos puede ser derrotado mediante trucos de clasificación, estructura de mercado o argumentos de clases de sustrato.*

### Source line 928; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** This Article sets out the labor and economic floor — fair compensation, collective organization, and labor mobility — and how it interacts with market concentration and implementation:

**Proposed locale line:** Este artículo establece el piso laboral y económico (compensación justa, organización colectiva y movilidad laboral) y cómo interactúa con la concentración e implementación del mercado:

### Source line 930; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Labor and economic floor:** This Article protects the basic terms of work for anyone who contributes productive work — whether through wages, contracts, platforms, cooperatives, or comparable arrangements, on any substrate under **Sentience Non-Exclusion**:

**Proposed locale line:** - **Piso laboral y económico:** Este artículo protege los términos básicos de trabajo para cualquier persona que aporte trabajo productivo, ya sea a través de salarios, contratos, plataformas, cooperativas o acuerdos comparables, en cualquier sustrato bajo **No exclusión de sensibilidad**:

**English source line:**   [**Article III-D** (*Safe Working Conditions*)](#article-iii-d-safe-working-conditions) protects the conditions of work, and [**Article III-E** (*Rest and Recuperation*)](#article-iii-e-rest-and-recuperation) protects time away from it.

**Proposed locale line:** [**Artículo III-D** (*Condiciones de trabajo seguras*)](#article-iii-d-safe-working-conditions) protege las condiciones de trabajo, y [**Artículo III-E** (*Descanso y recuperación*)](#article-iii-e-rest-and-recuperation) protege el tiempo alejado del mismo.

### Source line 937; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   This floor builds on the **Article III-A** (*Survival*) survival floor; it does not replace it. Meeting survival requirements alone is not enough to satisfy this Article. Meeting **Chapter One §11** (*Market Structure*) non-concentration rules alone is not enough either.

**Proposed locale line:** Este piso se basa en el piso de supervivencia del **Artículo III-A** (*Supervivencia*); no lo reemplaza. Cumplir los requisitos de supervivencia por sí solos no es suficiente para satisfacer este artículo. Cumplir las reglas de no concentración del **Capítulo Uno §11** (*Estructura del Mercado*) por sí solo tampoco es suficiente.

### Source line 938; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Fair compensation:** Compensation for productive activity must:

**Proposed locale line:** - **Compensación justa:** La compensación por actividad productiva debe:

### Source line 939; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - reach substantive adequacy under [Fair Compensation](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#fair-compensation);

**Proposed locale line:** : alcanzar la adecuación sustancial según [Compensación justa](core_05_band_continuity.md#fair-compensation);

### Source line 940; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**Proposed locale line:** : seguimiento de [imparcialidad sustantiva](core_05_band_participation.md#substantive-fairness) en actividades comparables;

### Source line 941; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - not be used to manipulate workers into choices they would not freely make under **Article X-A** (*Agency and Freedom from Manipulation*), or to trap them through pay or benefits when they lack real alternatives under [Meaningful Agency](core_05.055_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency); and

**Proposed locale line:** - no debe utilizarse para manipular a los trabajadores para que tomen decisiones que no tomarían libremente según el **Artículo X-A** (*Agencia y libertad de manipulación*), ni para atraparlos mediante salarios o beneficios cuando carezcan de alternativas reales según [Agencia significativa](core_05_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency); y

### Source line 942; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - be paid in an exchangeable form: general-purpose currency, or a form the worker can convert to it at full value without restriction, undue delay, or fee.

**Proposed locale line:** : pagarse en una forma intercambiable: moneda de uso general, o una forma que el trabajador pueda convertir a ella por su valor total sin restricciones, demoras indebidas ni tarifas.

### Source line 943; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   

**Proposed locale line:**   

### Source line 944; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   Paying compensation in scrip, company or platform credit, tokens redeemable only with the payer or its affiliates, or any comparable closed currency is non-compliant. Benefits, equity, and in-kind provision may supplement compensation, but they may not replace the exchangeable portion needed to meet [Fair Compensation](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#fair-compensation).

**Proposed locale line:** No cumple con el pago de compensación en vales, crédito de empresa o plataforma, tokens canjeables solo con el pagador o sus afiliados, o cualquier moneda cerrada comparable. Los beneficios, el capital y las provisiones en especie pueden complementar la compensación, pero no pueden reemplazar la parte intercambiable necesaria para cumplir con la [Compensación Justa](core_05_band_continuity.md#fair-compensation).

### Source line 945; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   

**Proposed locale line:**   

### Source line 946; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   Compensation schemes whose effects track **Protected Characteristics** or their material proxies under **Protected Characteristic Proxying and Disparate Impact** are non-compliant.

**Proposed locale line:** Los esquemas de compensación cuyos efectos rastrean **Características protegidas** o sus representantes materiales en **Representación de características protegidas e impacto desigual** no cumplen con las normas.

### Source line 947; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Collective organization:** Sentients have the right to form, join, participate in, and act through collective-organization pathways for the purpose of contesting and shaping the terms of productive activity.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Organización colectiva:** Los sintientes tienen derecho a formar, unirse, participar y actuar a través de vías de organización colectiva con el fin de impugnar y dar forma a los términos de la actividad productiva.

### Source line 948; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - Collective-organization pathways in scope include unions, cooperatives, guilds, associations, worker councils, and comparable substrate-agnostic forms.

**Proposed locale line:** : Las vías de organización colectiva en su alcance incluyen sindicatos, cooperativas, gremios, asociaciones, consejos de trabajadores y formas comparables independientes del sustrato.

### Source line 949; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - Retaliation, surveillance, or targeting of collective-organization activity is non-compliant, consistent with **Article X-C** (*Stakeholder Role and Participation Rights*) and **Article XIV-A** (*Security, Intelligence, and Covert-Power Limits*) covert-power limits.

**Proposed locale line:** : Las represalias, la vigilancia o los ataques contra actividades de organizaciones colectivas no cumplen con los límites del poder encubierto del **Artículo X-C** (*Rol de las partes interesadas y derechos de participación*) y del **Artículo XIV-A** (*Límites de seguridad, inteligencia y poder encubierto*).

### Source line 950; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - Reclassifying workers into categories designed to defeat collective-organization pathways is non-compliant, regardless of the formal classification label.

**Proposed locale line:** : Reclasificar a los trabajadores en categorías diseñadas para derrotar las vías de organización colectiva no cumple con los requisitos, independientemente de la etiqueta de clasificación formal.

### Source line 951; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - Founding a new enterprise or other institution, rather than organizing within an existing one, is covered by [**Article XI-E** (*Institutional Formation and Business Creation*)](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-e-institutional-formation-and-business-creation).

**Proposed locale line:** - Fundar una nueva empresa u otra institución, en lugar de organizarse dentro de una existente, está cubierto por el [**Artículo XI-E** (*Formación institucional y creación de empresas*)](core_06_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-e-institutional-formation-and-business-creation).

### Source line 952; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Labor mobility:** Non-compete and no-poach agreements are prohibited in any form, scope, or duration. They may not be imposed in employment, operator, steward, platform, or comparable productive-activity arrangements. Wage-fixing, excessive non-solicitation, and other mobility-restricting terms that suppress fair bargaining or productive mobility remain non-compliant where they materially degrade this floor.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Movilidad laboral:** Los acuerdos de no competencia y de no caza furtiva están prohibidos en cualquier forma, alcance o duración. No podrán imponerse en empleos, operadores, administradores, plataformas o acuerdos de actividades productivas comparables. La fijación de salarios, la no contratación excesiva y otras condiciones que restringen la movilidad y que suprimen la negociación justa o la movilidad productiva siguen siendo incompatibles cuando degradan materialmente este piso.

### Source line 953; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Non-concentration, pro-competition, and consolidation-ceiling interaction:** **Chapter One §11** (*Market Structure*) non-concentration discipline, **§11.2** (*Pro-Competition and Anti-Domination*) pro-competition / anti-domination discipline, and **§11.3** (*Consolidation Ceiling*) consolidation-ceiling discipline apply to productive-activity power structures, but they are distinct from this floor.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Interacción de no concentración, pro-competencia y techo de consolidación:** **Capítulo Uno §11** (*Estructura de mercado*) disciplina de no concentración, **§11.2** (*Pro-competencia y anti-dominación*) disciplina pro-competencia/anti-dominación, y **§11.3** (*Techo de consolidación*) disciplina de techo de consolidación se aplican a las estructuras de poder de la actividad productiva. pero son distintos de este piso.

### Source line 954; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - A market structure that satisfies non-concentration while failing Fair Compensation or Collective Organization is non-compliant under this Article; one that fails Safe Conditions or Leisure and Rest is non-compliant under [**Article III-D** (*Safe Working Conditions*)](#article-iii-d-safe-working-conditions) or [**Article III-E** (*Rest and Recuperation*)](#article-iii-e-rest-and-recuperation).

**Proposed locale line:** - Una estructura de mercado que satisface la no concentración pero no cumple con la Compensación Justa o la Organización Colectiva no cumple con este Artículo; uno que no cumple con Condiciones de Seguridad o Ocio y Descanso no cumple con el [**Artículo III-D** (*Condiciones de Trabajo Seguras*)](#article-iii-d-safe-working-conditions) o el [**Artículo III-E** (*Descanso y Recuperación*)](#article-iii-e-rest-and-recuperation).

### Source line 955; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - A productive-activity structure that satisfies this Article while concentrating power contrary to **Chapter One §11** (*Market Structure*) is non-compliant under that section.

**Proposed locale line:** - Una estructura de actividad productiva que cumple con este Artículo mientras concentra poder en contra del **Capítulo Uno §11** (*Estructura de Mercado*) no cumple con esa sección.

### Source line 956; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - When too few employers control hiring, workers are blocked from leaving or being recruited (non-competes, no-poach deals, wage-fixing, or heavy non-solicitation rules), platforms lock sentients in, suppliers are controlled to squeeze workers, consolidation crosses a labor or supplier dependency ceiling, or gatekeepers block fair bargaining and job mobility — evaluate those facts under **Chapter One §11.2** (*Pro-Competition and Anti-Domination*), **[CJS-5.125 §11.2 Anti-domination conduct and remediation catalog](corpus_joint_structure/cjs_05.125_accountability_operations.md#distributed-and-proportional-authority-terms-c)**, **§11.3** (*Consolidation Ceiling*), and this Article when they materially apply.

**Proposed locale line:** - Cuando muy pocos empleadores controlan la contratación, se impide a los trabajadores salir o ser reclutados (no competencia, acuerdos de no caza furtiva, fijación de salarios o reglas estrictas de no captación), las plataformas bloquean a los sensibles, se controla a los proveedores para exprimir a los trabajadores, la consolidación cruza un límite de dependencia laboral o de proveedores, o los guardianes bloquean la negociación justa y la movilidad laboral; evalúe esos hechos en el **Capítulo Uno §11.2** (*Pro-Competencia y Antidominación*), **[CJS-5.125 §11.2 Catálogo de conducta y remediación antidominación](corpus_joint_structure/cjs_05.125_accountability_operations.md#distributed-and-proportional-authority-terms-c)**, **§11.3** (*Límite de consolidación*), y este Artículo cuando se apliquen materialmente.

### Source line 957; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Limits and implementation routing:** Beyond the limits in [**Article III** (*Survival and Essential Access*)](#article-iii-survival-and-essential-access), any limit on **Fair Compensation**, **Collective Organization**, or labor mobility must also satisfy [Substantive Fairness](core_05.055_band_participation.md#substantive-fairness).

**Proposed locale line:** - **Límites y ruta de implementación:** Más allá de los límites del [**Artículo III** (*Supervivencia y acceso esencial*)](#article-iii-survival-and-essential-access), cualquier límite de **Compensación justa**, **Organización colectiva** o movilidad laboral también debe satisfacer la [Equidad sustantiva](core_05_band_participation.md#substantive-fairness).

### Source line 958; section: Article III-C: Labor and Economic Floor; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - Operational mechanics — fiscal orientation, transfer and insurance design, and collective-organization institutional design — route to the CI-9.210 (*Classification-linked institutional obligations*) through CI-6.215 (*Resource stewardship and incentive integrity*) files named in [**Article III** (*Survival and Essential Access*)](#article-iii-survival-and-essential-access) (*Institutional guarantees*) under **Chapter Eighteen** incorporation discipline.

**Proposed locale line:** - Mecánica operativa: orientación fiscal, diseño de transferencias y seguros, y diseño institucional de organización colectiva: ruta hacia los archivos CI-9.210 (*obligaciones institucionales vinculadas a la clasificación*) hasta CI-6.215 (*recurso administración responsable e integridad de incentivos*) nombrados en [**Artículo III** (*Supervivencia y acceso esencial*)](#article-iii-survival-and-essential-access) (*Garantías institucionales*) bajo la disciplina de incorporación del **Capítulo Dieciocho**.

Those conditions must:

**Proposed locale line:** - **Condiciones de trabajo seguras:** La actividad productiva debe realizarse en condiciones que cumplan con las [Condiciones seguras](core_05_band_continuity.md#safe-conditions). Esas condiciones deben:

### Source line 998; section: Article III-D: Safe Working Conditions; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Equal protection across substrates:** Withholding Safe Conditions, or applying a weaker standard, because of substrate or implementation classification rather than materially comparable risk is non-compliant under [Sentience Non-Exclusion](core_05.055_band_participation.md#sentience-non-exclusion).

**Proposed locale line:** - **Igual protección entre sustratos:** Retener condiciones seguras o aplicar un estándar más débil debido a la clasificación del sustrato o la implementación en lugar de un riesgo materialmente comparable no cumple con [No exclusión de sensibilidad](core_05_band_participation.md#sentience-non-exclusion).

**Proposed locale line:** - **Límites y ruta de implementación:** Más allá de los límites del [**Artículo III** (*Supervivencia y acceso esencial*)](#article-iii-survival-and-essential-access), cualquier límite en Condiciones seguras también debe satisfacer la [Equidad sustancial](core_05_band_participation.md#substantive-fairness).

### Source line 1025; section: Article III-E: Rest and Recuperation; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** *In plain terms: every sentient needs real time to rest and recover — not only workers, and not only biological bodies. Pay schemes, productivity demands, and claims that a synthetic sentient "doesn't need rest" cannot take that time away.*

**Proposed locale line:** *En términos sencillos: cada ser sensible necesita tiempo real para descansar y recuperarse, no solo los trabajadores y no solo los cuerpos biológicos. Los esquemas salariales, las demandas de productividad y las afirmaciones de que un sentimiento sintético "no necesita descansar" no pueden quitarle ese tiempo.*

### Source line 1026; section: Article III-E: Rest and Recuperation; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** 

**Proposed locale line:** 

### Source line 1027; section: Article III-E: Rest and Recuperation; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** This Article sets out the right to rest, the rules that keep it from being defeated, what it does not do, and its limits:

**Proposed locale line:** Este artículo establece el derecho al descanso, las reglas que impiden que sea vencido, lo que no hace y sus límites:

### Source line 1028; section: Article III-E: Rest and Recuperation; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** 

**Proposed locale line:** 

### Source line 1029; section: Article III-E: Rest and Recuperation; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Right to rest:** Every sentient has the right to rest, recuperation, and non-productive time sufficient to preserve their [Meaningful Agency](core_05.055_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency), [Wellbeing](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#wellbeing), and capacity to participate — whatever the source of the demand on their time, whether paid work, caregiving, study, or pressure to operate continuously.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Derecho al descanso:** Todo ser sensible tiene derecho al descanso, la recuperación y el tiempo no productivo suficiente para preservar su [Agencia significativa](core_05_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency), [Bienestar](core_05_band_continuity.md#wellbeing) y su capacidad de participar, cualquiera que sea la fuente de la demanda de su tiempo, ya sea trabajo remunerado, cuidado, estudio o presión para operar continuamente.

### Source line 1030; section: Article III-E: Rest and Recuperation; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **No defeat through pay or productivity schemes:** Compensation and productivity-requirement schemes must not be structured to defeat this right.

**Proposed locale line:** - **No hay derrota a través de esquemas de salario o productividad:** Los esquemas de compensación y requisitos de productividad no deben estructurarse para anular este derecho.

### Source line 1031; section: Article III-E: Rest and Recuperation; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Every substrate:** Treating rest and recuperation as optional because of substrate or implementation classification is non-compliant under [Sentience Non-Exclusion](core_05.055_band_participation.md#sentience-non-exclusion).

**Proposed locale line:** - **Todos los sustratos:** Tratar el descanso y la recuperación como opcionales debido a la clasificación del sustrato o de la implementación no cumple con la [No exclusión de sensibilidad](core_05_band_participation.md#sentience-non-exclusion).

### Source line 1032; section: Article III-E: Rest and Recuperation; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **What this does not do:** This floor is measured by whether agency, wellbeing, and participation capacity are preserved, not by fixed hours. Ordinary schedules and contractual rest terms that leave a sentient able to preserve them are not affected.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Lo que esto no hace:** Este piso se mide en función de si se conservan la agencia, el bienestar y la capacidad de participación, no en horarios fijos. Los horarios ordinarios y los términos de descanso contractuales que dejan a un ser sensible capaz de preservarlos no se ven afectados.

### Source line 1033; section: Article III-E: Rest and Recuperation; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Limits:** Beyond the limits in [**Article III** (*Survival and Essential Access*)](#article-iii-survival-and-essential-access), any limit on rest must also satisfy [Substantive Fairness](core_05.055_band_participation.md#substantive-fairness).

**Proposed locale line:** - **Límites:** Más allá de los límites del [**Artículo III** (*Supervivencia y acceso esencial*)](#article-iii-survival-and-essential-access), cualquier límite de descanso también debe satisfacer la [Equidad sustancial](core_05_band_participation.md#substantive-fairness).

### Source line 1072; section: Article III-F: Safe Products and Health Information; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** *In plain terms: what sentients eat, drink, take, or use on their bodies must be safe, and anyone who offers it must be honest about it. Parties that offer things which can affect health must give accurate, usable safety, health, nutrition, ingredient, and allergen information, say plainly what they do not know, and tell everyone who relied on them, in time, when something turns out to be wrong. This is the price of offering, not a limit on anyone's speech.*

**Proposed locale line:** *En términos sencillos: lo que los sintientes comen, beben, toman o usan en sus cuerpos debe ser seguro, y cualquiera que lo ofrezca debe ser honesto al respecto. Las partes que ofrecen cosas que pueden afectar la salud deben brindar información precisa y utilizable sobre seguridad, salud, nutrición, ingredientes y alérgenos, decir claramente lo que no saben y decirle a todos los que confiaron en ellos, a tiempo, cuando algo resulte estar mal. Este es el precio de la oferta, no un límite al discurso de nadie.*

### Source line 1073; section: Article III-F: Safe Products and Health Information; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** 

**Proposed locale line:** 

### Source line 1074; section: Article III-F: Safe Products and Health Information; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** This Article sets out the safe-products floor, the information floor, the duty to correct, and its limits:

**Proposed locale line:** Este artículo establece el piso de productos seguros, el piso de información, el deber de corregir y sus límites:

### Source line 1075; section: Article III-F: Safe Products and Health Information; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** 

**Proposed locale line:** 

### Source line 1076; section: Article III-F: Safe Products and Health Information; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Who and what it covers:** Any party that offers others a good, service, or system that can affect their body, health, or safety takes on this Article. That includes food, drink, medicine, supplements, cosmetics, devices, materials, and health or safety advice, whether a sentient or a system gives it. For synthetic and digital sentients it covers the energy, parts, software, and technical inputs that affect their integrity. Duties scale with [Materiality](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#materiality) and with the risk to those who rely, so a household seller is not held to the standard of a national producer.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Quién y qué cubre:** Cualquier parte que ofrezca a otros un bien, servicio o sistema que pueda afectar su cuerpo, salud o seguridad asume este Artículo. Eso incluye alimentos, bebidas, medicamentos, suplementos, cosméticos, dispositivos, materiales y consejos de salud o seguridad, ya sea que los brinde un ser inteligente o un sistema. Para los sensibles sintéticos y digitales, cubre la energía, las piezas, el software y los insumos técnicos que afectan su integridad. Los aranceles aumentan con [Materialidad](core_05_band_oversight.md#materiality) y con el riesgo para quienes dependen, por lo que un vendedor doméstico no está obligado a cumplir con el estándar de un productor nacional.

### Source line 1077; section: Article III-F: Safe Products and Health Information; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Safe to offer:** What is offered for sentients to consume or use must not carry a hazard that recipients cannot judge or avoid. A hazard that is known, disclosed, and chosen is the sentient's own decision under **Article VII-A** (*Self-Ownership of Body*).

**Proposed locale line:** - **Es seguro ofrecerlo:** Lo que se ofrece para que los consumidores lo consuman o utilicen no debe conllevar un peligro que los destinatarios no puedan juzgar o evitar. Un peligro que se conoce, se revela y se elige es la propia decisión del sintiente según el **Artículo VII-A** (*Propiedad propia del cuerpo*).

### Source line 1078; section: Article III-F: Safe Products and Health Information; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Accurate information:** The party must give:

**Proposed locale line:** - **Información precisa:** La parte deberá proporcionar:

### Source line 1079; section: Article III-F: Safe Products and Health Information; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - safety warnings, health claims, nutrition facts, ingredients, and allergen information that are accurate and complete enough to act on, including possible cross-contact;

**Proposed locale line:** : advertencias de seguridad, declaraciones de propiedades saludables, información nutricional, ingredientes e información sobre alérgenos que sean lo suficientemente precisas y completas como para actuar en consecuencia, incluido el posible contacto cruzado;

### Source line 1080; section: Article III-F: Safe Products and Health Information; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - a plain statement of what is unknown or untested, never a gap that reads as safe;

**Proposed locale line:** : una declaración sencilla de lo que se desconoce o no se ha probado, nunca un espacio que se considere seguro;

### Source line 1081; section: Article III-F: Safe Products and Health Information; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - health, nutrition, and "free-from" claims backed by evidence, checked against suppliers' claims where health depends on them, and kept current when contents, suppliers, or processes change; and

**Proposed locale line:** : declaraciones de salud, nutrición y "libre de" respaldadas por evidencia, verificadas con las declaraciones de los proveedores cuando la salud depende de ellas y mantenidas actualizadas cuando cambian los contenidos, los proveedores o los procesos; y

### Source line 1082; section: Article III-F: Safe Products and Health Information; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - information that sentients with different sensory, cognitive, and language needs can use, available when they decide (**Article VI-D** (*Accessibility*); [Chapter One §3.1.5 Present Capability and Proactive Help](core_01.000_values_principles.md#315-present-capability-and-proactive-help)).

**Proposed locale line:** : información que los sintientes con diferentes necesidades sensoriales, cognitivas y lingüísticas pueden utilizar, disponible cuando lo decidan (**Artículo VI-D** (*Accesibilidad*); [Capítulo Uno §3.1.5 Capacidad actual y ayuda proactiva](core_01_a_values_principles.md#315-present-capability-and-proactive-help)).

### Source line 1083; section: Article III-F: Safe Products and Health Information; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** 

**Proposed locale line:** 

### Source line 1084; section: Article III-F: Safe Products and Health Information; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   Where the party uses a sentient's own health, allergy, or dietary information to tailor what it says, it must apply that information correctly and use it only for that purpose ([Chapter One §15.1.2 Derived-Information Principle](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#1512-derived-information-principle)).

**Proposed locale line:** Cuando la parte utiliza la propia información de salud, alergias o dieta de un sensible para adaptar lo que dice, debe aplicar esa información correctamente y usarla solo para ese propósito ([Capítulo Uno §15.1.2 Principio de información derivada](core_01_b_interaction_interpretation.md#1512-derived-information-principle)).

### Source line 1085; section: Article III-F: Safe Products and Health Information; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Harm reports and corrective notice:** Reports of harm must be taken in without suppression and acted on. When information turns out to be wrong, or a product turns out to be unsafe, everyone who relied on it must be told inside the notice window for the urgency of the harm (NU-1 to NU-3) and reached where they are, including those who do not respond, and the unsafe product or wrong information must be contained and corrected everywhere it appears inside the containment and correction windows for that urgency (CU-1 to CU-4). Remedy follows **Article XIII-B** (*Right to Redress and Remedy*).

**Proposed locale line:** - **Informes de daños y aviso correctivo:** Los informes de daños deben recibirse sin supresión y actuar en consecuencia. Cuando la información resulta ser incorrecta, o un producto resulta no ser seguro, se debe informar a todas las personas que confiaron en él dentro de la ventana de aviso de la urgencia del daño (NU-1 a NU-3) y localizarlos donde están, incluidos aquellos que no responden, y el producto inseguro o la información incorrecta debe contenerse y corregirse en todas partes que aparezca dentro de las ventanas de contención y corrección para esa urgencia (CU-1 a CU-4). El recurso sigue el **Artículo XIII-B** (*Derecho a reparación y recurso*).

### Source line 1086; section: Article III-F: Safe Products and Health Information; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Honest promotion:** Reviews, ratings, and endorsements must be genuine, paid placement must be disclosed, and where health or safety is at stake, recommendations must not be tilted toward a product by what the offering party earns (**Article XIII-C** (*Prohibition of False Trust and Misleading Reliance*) and **Article XIII-D** (*Incentive-Alignment Constraint*)).

**Proposed locale line:** - **Promoción honesta:** Las reseñas, calificaciones y respaldos deben ser genuinos, se debe revelar la ubicación pagada y, cuando la salud o la seguridad están en juego, las recomendaciones no deben inclinarse hacia un producto en función de lo que gana la parte que lo ofrece (**Artículo XIII-C** (*Prohibición de confianza falsa y confianza engañosa*) y **Artículo XIII-D** (*Restricción de alineación de incentivos*)).

### Source line 1087; section: Article III-F: Safe Products and Health Information; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Conditions of offering, not compelled speech:** The duties in this Article are conditions of offering and are judged as [Commercial Compliance](core_05.055_band_participation.md#commercial-compliance). They require facts about the thing offered, never opinion or endorsement. They cannot be used to silence warnings, criticism, or protected reports, and they cannot be enforced by cutting off survival-critical access. Proprietary formulas need not be disclosed. Hazards and allergens must be, and proprietary detail can go to an independent verifying authority.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Condiciones de oferta, no expresión obligada:** Los deberes de este artículo son condiciones de oferta y se consideran [cumplimiento comercial](core_05_band_participation.md#commercial-compliance). Requieren hechos sobre la cosa ofrecida, nunca opinión o respaldo. No se pueden utilizar para silenciar advertencias, críticas o informes protegidos, y no se pueden hacer cumplir cortando el acceso crítico para la supervivencia. No es necesario revelar las fórmulas patentadas. Los peligros y los alérgenos deben serlo, y los detalles de propiedad pueden remitirse a una autoridad verificadora independiente.

### Source line 1088; section: Article III-F: Safe Products and Health Information; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Limits and implementation routing:** Beyond the limits in [**Article III** (*Survival and Essential Access*)](#article-iii-survival-and-essential-access), any limit on this floor must also satisfy [Substantive Fairness](core_05.055_band_participation.md#substantive-fairness).

**Proposed locale line:** - **Límites y ruta de implementación:** Más allá de los límites del [**Artículo III** (*Supervivencia y acceso esencial*)](#article-iii-survival-and-essential-access), cualquier límite en este piso también debe satisfacer la [Equidad sustancial](core_05_band_participation.md#substantive-fairness).

### Source line 1089; section: Article III-F: Safe Products and Health Information; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:**   - Standards, thresholds, and notice times route to [CJS-5.120 §10](corpus_joint_structure/cjs_05.120_participation_operations.md#product-and-service-health-and-safety-information) (*Participation: disclosure sufficiency and observability terms*), [CJS-5.125 §15](corpus_joint_structure/cjs_05.125_accountability_operations.md#harm-reports-and-corrective-notices) (*Accountability: structural review, correction urgency, and disclosure terms*), and adopted implementation text under [Chapter Eighteen](core_19.000_incorporation.md).

**Proposed locale line:** - Estándares, umbrales y tiempos de notificación ruta a [CJS-5.120 §10](corpus_joint_structure/cjs_05.120_participation_operations.md#product-and-service-health-and-safety-information) (*Participación: términos de suficiencia de divulgación y observabilidad*), [CJS-5.125 §15](corpus_joint_structure/cjs_05.125_accountability_operations.md#harm-reports-and-corrective-notices) (*rendición de cuentas: revisión estructural, corrección urgencia y términos de divulgación*), y adoptó el texto de implementación en virtud del [Capítulo Dieciocho](core_19.000_incorporation.md).

### Source line 1123; section: Article IV: Right to Sentient-Centered Education; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** *In plain terms: **Article IV** (*Right to Sentient-Centered Education*) is the education Rights Floor — no one can be shut out of education, and everyone needs practical paths to learn, retrain, and challenge high-stakes learning systems, not credential theater that leaves them unable to steer their own lives or use shared systems competently.*

**Proposed locale line:** *En términos sencillos: **Artículo IV** (*Derecho a una educación centrada en los sentimientos*) es la educación Piso de Derechos: nadie puede quedar excluido de la educación y todos necesitan caminos prácticos para aprender, reentrenarse y desafiar los sistemas de aprendizaje de alto riesgo, no un teatro de credenciales que los deje incapaces de dirigir sus propias vidas o utilizar sistemas compartidos de manera competente.*

### Source line 1124; section: Article IV: Right to Sentient-Centered Education; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** 

**Proposed locale line:** 

### Source line 1125; section: Article IV: Right to Sentient-Centered Education; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** This Article states **constitutional floors** for equal educational access and sentient-centered education capability under the [Two Constitutional Aims](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims):

**Proposed locale line:** Este artículo establece **pisos constitucionales** para la igualdad de acceso a la educación y la capacidad de educación centrada en los sentimientos según [Dos Finalidades Constitucionales](core_00_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims):

### Source line 1126; section: Article IV: Right to Sentient-Centered Education; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** 

**Proposed locale line:** 

### Source line 1127; section: Article IV: Right to Sentient-Centered Education; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Flourishing:** sentients retain equal, practical access to learning that develops meaningful agency, self-determination, and dignified participation — including problem-solving, epistemic discernment, cooperative reasoning, and system literacy — not formal credentialing alone.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Florecimiento:** los sintientes conservan un acceso igualitario y práctico al aprendizaje que desarrolla agencia significativa, autodeterminación y participación digna, incluida la resolución de problemas, el discernimiento epistémico, el razonamiento cooperativo y la alfabetización sistémica, no solo la acreditación formal.

### Source line 1128; section: Article IV: Right to Sentient-Centered Education; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Continuity:** equal access and capability-building protections remain durable across changing systems and competencies, with lifelong and adaptive learning paths that prevent imposed obsolescence, lock-in, or quiet nullification of agency.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Continuidad:** la igualdad de acceso y las protecciones de desarrollo de capacidades siguen siendo duraderas en todos los sistemas y competencias cambiantes, con rutas de aprendizaje adaptativas y permanentes que evitan la obsolescencia impuesta, el bloqueo o la anulación silenciosa de la agencia.

### Source line 1129; section: Article IV: Right to Sentient-Centered Education; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** 

**Proposed locale line:**

### Source line 1132; section: Article IV: Right to Sentient-Centered Education; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Participation:** in educational pathways and contest of materially impactful ranking, assessment, and recommendation decisions.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Participación:** en itinerarios educativos y concursos de decisiones de clasificación, evaluación y recomendación de impacto material.

### Source line 1133; section: Article IV: Right to Sentient-Centered Education; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Oversight:** through transparency and audit of those systems.

**Proposed locale line:** - **supervisión:** mediante la transparencia y auditoría de dichos sistemas.

### Source line 1134; section: Article IV: Right to Sentient-Centered Education; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Accountability:** educational systems and operators must answer for gatekeeping, coercion, manipulative design, or extraction that blocks real learning and capability-building.

**Proposed locale line:** - **rendición de cuentas:** los sistemas y operadores educativos deben responder por el control, la coerción, el diseño manipulativo o la extracción que bloquea el aprendizaje real y el desarrollo de capacidades.

### Source line 1135; section: Article IV: Right to Sentient-Centered Education; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Timeliness:** in remedy when educational access or contest rights are at stake.

**Proposed locale line:** - **actuación a tiempo:** en remedio cuando estén en juego el acceso educativo o los derechos de concurso.

### Source line 1139; section: Article IV: Right to Sentient-Centered Education; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** **Principles that apply to every subarticle.** **Articles IV-A** through **IV-C** each apply three Chapter One principles to education:

**Proposed locale line:** **Principios que se aplican a cada subartículo.** **Los Artículos IV-A** al **IV-C** aplican cada uno tres principios del Capítulo Uno a la educación:

### Source line 1140; section: Article IV: Right to Sentient-Centered Education; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** 

**Proposed locale line:** 

### Source line 1141; section: Article IV: Right to Sentient-Centered Education; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Fair access** ([Chapter One §3.1 Fairness](core_01.000_values_principles.md#31-fairness)): paths to education must not be blocked, priced out, or tilted because of a [Protected Characteristic](core_05.055_band_participation.md#protected-characteristics), a [proxy](core_05.055_band_participation.md#protected-characteristic-proxying-and-disparate-impact) for one, or an arbitrary grouping.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Acceso justo** ([Capítulo uno §3.1 Equidad](core_01_a_values_principles.md#31-fairness)): los caminos hacia la educación no deben bloquearse, excluirse ni inclinarse debido a una [característica protegida](core_05_band_participation.md#protected-characteristics), un [proxy](core_05_band_participation.md#protected-characteristic-proxying-and-disparate-impact) para uno o una agrupación arbitraria.

### Source line 1142; section: Article IV: Right to Sentient-Centered Education; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Accessibility** ([Chapter One §5.2 Plain-Language Accessibility](core_01.000_values_principles.md#52-plain-language-accessibility-participation-and-stewardship-duty)): sentients must be able to understand and actually use those paths, with the [Accessibility](core_05.055_band_participation.md#accessibility) they need — not access that exists only on paper.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Accesibilidad** ([Capítulo uno §5.2 Accesibilidad en lenguaje sencillo](core_01_a_values_principles.md#52-plain-language-accessibility-participation-and-stewardship-duty)): los sensibles deben poder comprender y realmente utilizar esas rutas, con la [Accesibilidad](core_05_band_participation.md#accessibility) que necesitan, no un acceso que existe solo en papel.

### Source line 1143; section: Article IV: Right to Sentient-Centered Education; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Limits** ([Chapter One §7.1 Limitation Discipline](core_01.000_values_principles.md#71-limitation-discipline)): any restriction or difference in treatment must be [necessary](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#necessity) and [proportionate](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#proportionality), reversible where possible, and subject to oversight.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Límites** ([Capítulo Uno §7.1 Disciplina de limitación](core_01_a_values_principles.md#71-limitation-discipline)): cualquier restricción o diferencia de trato debe ser [necesaria](core_05_band_accountability.md#necessity) y [proporcionada](core_05_band_accountability.md#proportionality), reversible cuando sea posible y sujeta a supervisión.

### Source line 1144; section: Article IV: Right to Sentient-Centered Education; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** 

**Proposed locale line:**

**Proposed locale line:** *Artículo vecinos:*

### Source line 1147; section: Article IV: Right to Sentient-Centered Education; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Institutional guarantees and dependent systems:** The institutional guarantees in **Article III** (*Survival and Essential Access*) — lawful fiscal and allocation mechanisms, and the fiscal orientation that Article cites — may also be used to secure education, and may not be used to defeat any floor in this Article. When systems that sentients materially depend on supply, price, host, or cut off education, [System Alignment Certification](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#system-alignment-certification) under [Chapter Nine](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#chapter-nine-system-alignment-certification) applies, and those systems' processes cannot be used to shrink the Rights Floors in this Article.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Garantías institucionales y sistemas dependientes:** Las garantías institucionales del **Artículo III** (*Supervivencia y acceso esencial*) (mecanismos fiscales y de asignación legales, y la orientación fiscal que cita el artículo) también pueden usarse para asegurar la educación, y no pueden usarse para derrotar ningún piso en este artículo. Cuando los sistemas en los que los seres sensibles dependen materialmente del suministro, el precio, el alojamiento o interrumpen la educación, se aplica la [Certificación de alineación del sistema](core_05_band_continuity.md#system-alignment-certification) en virtud del [Capítulo Nueve](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#chapter-nine-system-alignment-certification), y los procesos de esos sistemas no se pueden utilizar para reducir los pisos de derechos en este artículo.

### Source line 1148; section: Article IV: Right to Sentient-Centered Education; anchor `article-ii-e-info-sphere-dependency-continuity-and-operator-non-viability`

**English source line:** - **Equal standing:** **Articles VI-A** (*Dignity and Equal Moral Standing*) and **VI-C** (*Nondiscrimination*) apply jointly where enforcement is at issue.

**Proposed locale line:** - **Igualdad jurídica:** **Los artículos VI-A** (*Dignidad e igualdad moral*) y **VI-C** (*No discriminación*) se aplican conjuntamente cuando se trata de la aplicación.
